### Amendment 5 (2026-11-13: AlphaGenome 2 as a competitor)

Fifth planner call-back, umbrella at ef4a206. It applies the PI task of 2026-11-13 (`cruxvault/INSTRUCTIONS/peeling_the_onion/AlphaGenome 2.md` in the main checkout): AlphaGenome 2 (AG2) enters the run as a competitor, queried through its API. It changes no finished chunk, no PI ruling, no decision D1 to D16, no Log line and no existing chunk section. Amended once in place after the cold review `.orchestrate/review_A5.md` and PI rulings 34 to 37 (2026-11-13): every blocking finding is fixed; the declined minors are listed at the end of section D. Where it touches an existing chunk it says "addendum to the foreman's brief of chunk X". Every chunk below is `task: new`. Chunk sections of this amendment are the `####` headings of this section. Names: `K`, `O`, `M`, `T1`, `T2`, `EV`, `R`, `PT`, `RC` as in "Names used in the amendment 2 sections". New names: `AGD=$O/ag2` (Nibi; every AG2 file of this run), `AGV=/project/def-maxwl/mforooz/ONION/envs/ag2` (the client venv, `alphagenome` 2.0.0), `AGK=~/.secrets/alphagenome_api_key` (Nibi, mode 600), `MAXP=/project/def-maxwl/maxwl/epi_imputation/s12/fair` (Fir, Max's files, read only), `MAXR=mlibbrecht/01_2026-10-19_research` at commit `bde905a`, folder `projects/03_2026-10-20_epi_imputation/` (Max's code; "S11" and "S12" are its sprint folders 11 and 12). "Condition" means one AG2 reference condition, i.e. one row of AG2's learned table of cell states. "Channel" means one AG2 output track type, i.e. (assay, ChIP target, read type). "Track status" means the label that the leakage audit gives one of our tracks. "Method track files" means the per-target arrays of pinned interface F below.

**Inputs read, and what was verified (2026-11-13).** `cruxvault/results/ag2/AG2_FACTS.md` and `cruxvault/results/ag2/MAX_SPRINT12.md`, in full. In code: `tools/onion_eval/signal_from_counts.py` (`pileup_from_counts` :140, `macs2_lambda` :147, `signal_track` :256, `signal_from_nb` :306); `tools/onion_eval/score.py` (`Scorer._signal_target` :1193 holds the truth and the predicted signal of a target per bin; `_override_signal` :1283; no mask is applied to the signal-space metrics, the only mask is the region choice of `evalset.py`; `--lambda-source` :2039 with `predicted_control`; `ledger_append` :1890; `LEDGER_COLUMNS` :247); `tools/onion_eval/external_signal.py` (`cmd_score` :225 refuses an entry that is not one of `eic_score.TEAMS`); `slurm/onion_eval/score.sh:50` and `external_signal.sh:49` pass trailing arguments on; `src/candi/bench/binary.py:26` has `average_precision`; `tools/onion_data/encode_catalog.py:80-82` writes `biosample_term_id` per experiment; `tools/onion_data/build_list.py:54,118` gives signal products (`signal.h5`, `pr1_signal.h5`, `pr2_signal.h5`) to validation and blind tracks only; `tools/onion_data/fastq_uniform.py:11` calls no peaks. From `cruxvault/results/t158/accession_ledger.tsv` (436 rows): non-control tracks are 267 training, 45 validation, 51 blind; cells are 51 in training, 26 in validation, 12 in blind; all 51 blind tracks are paired-end, 43 of 45 validation tracks are single-end. No file of this run holds an AG2 output yet, and no AG2 metadata. Not verified: Max's Fir paths (his notes state them; chunk AGm checks them on disk) and the API facts that the smoke of 2026-11-12 did not cover (chunk AG0). Known from that smoke: the client works from a Nibi login node in `$AGV`; `observed_metadata(HOMO_SAPIENS)` returned chip_histone (1183, 12), dnase (385, 11), atac (179, 11).

**A fact found while planning (the evidence notes do not state it).** The 12 EIC blind cells are BE2C, BMEC, Caco-2, DND-41, HAP-1, HL-60, MG63, NCI-H929, RWPE2, SJCRH30, SJSA1 and WERI-Rb-1 (ledger). Eleven of them are, by name, among the 32 conditions with held-out tracks in the AG2 paper's imputation benchmark (`AG2_FACTS.md` section (c)), and the twelfth (BMEC) probably is the paper's "brain endothelium". So the paper's held-out test tracks probably include EIC blind tracks, and the statement "AG2 does not report results on EIC tracks" is true in name only. The API serves only the model that trained on every observed track. The expected result of the audit is therefore that the API model trained on most or all EIC blind tracks. The audit (chunk AGa) measures it. Under PI ruling 34 such tracks form the second, labelled comparison.

**PI rulings applied (2026-11-13; they replace the former decisions (o) to (r)).** (34) The headline is the same-track comparison on tracks that AG2 never trained on. A second comparison is required, not optional: tracks that are in AG2's training data and that the count model did not train on (for example the EIC blind tracks, inside the single ledgered blind pass). The PI expects AG2 to win it by a large margin. It is reported with the trained-on label and is never the headline. Reason: AG2 is not open weight and cannot be retrained, so a test on its training data puts CANDI at a disadvantage. (34b) The test set of the extended dataset (D11) must hold enough tracks outside AG2's training set for a fair comparison; recorded as a requirement of gate G5 (addendum to chunk G5x, section C). (35) AG2 gets no row on the EIC challenge board; it gets a separate board on our evaluation regions. (36) No scRNA-seq conditioning; such cells are reported as not queryable. (37) "Leaderboard" means this run's comparison tables plus the new AG2 board; `tools/leaderboard.py` is untouched. Terms of Service: the PI read them; publishing benchmark results from API outputs is allowed for non-commercial research; AG2 outputs stay evaluation-only and raw AG2 tracks are not redistributed.

**A. Decisions applied.**

Decided by Max (source: `MAX_SPRINT12.md` section 2, which cites his files). We follow each one.

| fork | decision | source |
|---|---|---|
| comparison frame | same tracks only; a paper number is never set beside one of ours | S11 `INSTRUCTIONS.md` l.7 (Max's words) |
| AG2 mode | DNA plus a reference condition, released model, no scRNA-seq conditioning | S11 `scripts/api/fetch_ag2.py` l.1-16 |
| fairness | a track is unseen when AG2 has no training track for that condition and mark with either read type; status from `observed_metadata()` | S12 `scripts/split/make_split_f1.py` l.2-23 |
| condition mapping | ENCODE ontology term equal to the AG2 condition; else the composite condition with the most ENCODE bulk tracks in AG2 training | `make_split_f1.py` l.11-17 |
| channel | the channel of the target's mark and read type ("matched"); the other read type is scored too; if no matched channel exists the other one is used | S12 `scripts/api/score_f1.py` l.17-18 |
| truth and units | truth = arcsinh(-log10 p) at 25 bp; AG2 = raw fold change, no log, no pseudocount; every method gets an oracle monotone recalibration to the truth (isotonic, 2,000 quantile bins, fitted on the track itself) | `score_f1.py` l.20-24 |
| resolution | AG2 128 bp to 25 bp by an overlap-weighted mean; scoring at 25 bp | `score_f1.py` `to25` |
| tiling | 1 Mb windows; position inside the window does not matter (r >= 0.999) | S11 `fetch_ag2.py` l.4-8; `context_check.tsv` |
| metrics | primary `rspec_cal` (r_spec after recalibration); secondary `pearson_cal`, Spearman, consensus-peak AUPRC overall and in 8 peak-frequency bins, and the uncalibrated r_spec and Pearson | `score_f1.py` l.20-24; `summarize_f1.py` l.13-17 |
| peaks | ENCODE peak calls on 500 bp tiles (20 bins); a tile is "consensus" when any profiled track of the assay has a peak bin in it; positive when the target has one; score = mean predicted signal of the tile | S11 `scripts/bridge/peak_tiles.py` l.2-13 |
| baselines | the training average of the assay; the closest-5 oracle (per 200 kb block, the mean of the 5 training tracks most correlated with the truth) | `score_f1.py` l.13-19 |
| statistics | per-target difference A - B; 95% interval from a cluster bootstrap over cell-type terms, 2,000 resamples; a difference is called only when the interval excludes 0; the contrast is written down before any number | `summarize_f1.py` l.15-17 |
| API calls | client 2.0.0; chunks of 25 intervals, 5 workers; backoff 30 s x 2^k, at most 600 s, 8 tries; restartable cache; float16 clipped at 6e4 | S11 `fetch_ag2.py` l.17-66 |
| asymmetries accepted | the count model reads the cell's other tracks at the test positions; AG2 trained on the test positions for its other tracks | S12 sprint log l.25 |

Decided here (code and supplementary level; the foreman logs them in the notepad). Max left each of these open or tied it to his own data.
1. Regions. The AG2 comparison is scored on the regions of the frozen eval set `$EV` (256 regions of 16,384 bins, 3.46% of chr1 to chr22 and chrX), not on chr20 to chr22. Reason: every onion score, noise floor and baseline lives on these regions, and chr20 to chr22 were Max's choice because his store holds them out; miniCANDI holds out tracks, as AG2 does. chr20 to chr22 are used only by the reproduction gate (chunk AGm), on Max's cached predictions.
2. Truth. The truth is the onion `signal_arcsinh` truth (asinh of `signal.h5`, the uniform pipeline's -log10 p track of this run), which is Max's truth space on our reprocessed data.
3. Count model. "CANDI" in every table of this amendment is miniCANDI (`final` on validation tracks, `final` and `final_tv` on blind tracks). The full CANDI against AG2 is Max's Benchmark F1; it is cited, never rebuilt here. Regime `leave_one_assay_out` corresponds to Max's input setting "all" and is the headline; `accessibility_only` corresponds to his "primary" and is the second table.
4. Model checkpoint: `ALL_FOLDS` only (Max). `FOLD_1` is not fetched. Reason: it answers a held-out-sequence question, and it doubles the calls.
5. Fold change from counts uses no pseudocount and the lambda of D9b (section B.2). Reason: ENCODE's fold-change track is `bdgcmp -m FE` with pseudocount 0, and lambda is never below the genome background, so no division by 0 occurs.
6. Blacklist and mask: none beyond the region choice. `score.py` applies no bin mask to its signal-space metrics (`_signal_target` calls `r_spec` on all bins of the regions); the eval regions already have a genome-mask valid fraction of at least 0.9. Every row of the AG2 board uses all bins of the regions. Reason: one rule for all rows, and the rule of the onion signal metrics.
7. Count-space and distribution metrics for AG2: none. AG2 gives a point value and no covariate output, so headline metrics 3 to 7 print `not applicable (no distribution, no covariate output)` on its row. Reason: converting fold change to counts needs our control and depth, and would test our map, not AG2.
8. Replicate floor: one reference row `pseudoreplicate_half`: the target's `pr1_signal.h5` scored against its `pr2_signal.h5` as the truth (two halves with no shared read; a half scored against the pooled track would be inflated by its own reads). It is a floor at half depth, so it is a lower bound of the ceiling at full depth; the table says so. Reason: `AGENTS.md` section 7.2 needs a noise floor beside every number; Max's tables have none.
9. Avocado and the other EIC entries: their published tracks, on blind tracks only, through the existing route of chunk XE. Nothing is retrained (D16).
10. Assays: histone ChIP-seq, DNase-seq and ATAC-seq are audited. A DNase-seq or ATAC-seq track is fetched and scored only if the audit finds it unseen. The EIC panel has no transcription-factor track.
11. ChIP-seq control. The D9b signal of a count model reads the target cell's observed control for lambda in every regime (`signal_from_nb`), and the truth `signal.h5` used the same control. AG2 gets no control. So the headline table (`leave_one_assay_out`, observed control) carries the label `uses_target_cell_control = yes` on count-model rows, and this is an accepted asymmetry, listed as such. The control-free table is `accessibility_only` scored with `--lambda-source predicted_control` (the model's predicted control; for a baseline the scorer's training-average control), label `uses_target_cell_control = no`. The question that Max left open is read off these two tables. On blind tracks only the headline table exists, because the single pass runs with the frozen lambda source.
12. AG2 outputs are evaluation data only. They are never an input, a training target, a baseline input or a blend member (README terms; D10). They live under `$AGD`, outside every tables directory, and each pack directory holds the marker file `EVALUATION_ONLY`.
13. The repository-level rivals leaderboard (`tools/leaderboard.py`, `leaderboard/boards.json`) is not touched, and "leaderboard" in this run is the pair of tables of chunk XCb plus the new table `ag2_board` (section B.5). Confirmed by PI ruling 37.
14. The API key is read only from the file named by the environment variable `AG2_KEY_FILE` (default `$AGK`). A tool refuses a key file whose mode is not 600. A tool also refuses a key file that does not hold exactly one non-empty line. No tool takes a key as an argument, prints it, or writes it. Error text from the API is logged as its status code name only, because Max's raw log quotes the account's project number.
15. Max's cached predictions are used only for the reproduction gate. Reason: they cover his Dataset 1 targets on chr20 to chr22, not the EIC tracks on our regions.
16. No selection reads AG2. No D13 settlement, mix-and-match choice, evolution step, stage-2 or stage-3 choice, ruling-14 arm or freeze decision reads a file under `$AGD` or a result of chunks AGva to AGz. AG2 validation numbers are reported only. Reason: the validation AG2 result exists from wave 9 on, before the ladder settles, and AG2 outputs are evaluation-only.
17. The binary peak call uses one rule for every row (section B.3). Reason: a threshold that only AG2 gets would favor it.
18. The wiki page `cruxvault/wiki/alphagenome-2.md` and the edited wiki files (uncommitted in the working tree) are committed by the foreman on the umbrella branch; no chunk owns them.

**How this sits with D16.** D16 says that external methods are compared on EIC only, against their published submissions, and that no external method is retrained in this run. AG2 is an external method. It is not retrained, and it is compared on EIC tracks only, so both of those clauses hold. AG2 has no published EIC submission, so the PI's task of 2026-11-13 extends the words "against their published submissions" in one way: AG2 enters through API predictions for EIC tracks, with a track status per row. No other part of D16 moves.

**B. The design.**

**B.1 Leakage audit.** Unit: one EIC track (ledger row, non-control), 363 rows = 267 training + 45 validation + 51 blind. Inputs: `accession_ledger.tsv`; `$O/catalog/encode/experiments.tsv` (`biosample_term_id` per experiment); the normalized AG2 metadata of chunk AG0 (pinned interface G). Rule (Max's): map the track's `biosample_term_id` to a condition (equal CURIE; else the composite condition that contains the term and has the most ENCODE bulk tracks in AG2 training; else none). A "composite condition" is a condition whose `biosample_curies` field holds more than one CURIE. "ENCODE bulk tracks" of a condition are its rows of `ag2_observed.tsv` whose `data_source` is ENCODE (the value that `META_SCHEMA.md` records) and whose `output_type` is `CHIP_HISTONE`, `DNASE` or `ATAC`. Then the track status, one of:
- `observed_matched`: AG2 has an observed training track for the condition, the mark (or DNase-seq, ATAC-seq) and the target's read type.
- `observed_other_read_type`: observed for the other read type only.
- `sibling_condition`: the mapped condition has no such track, and another condition of the same ENCODE term has one. Set aside, as Max does.
- `unseen`: the condition exists and has no observed track of that mark with either read type. The API label is `imputed_supported` when the condition has an observed accessibility track, else `imputed_exploratory` (Docs rule; the fetch stores the label that the API returns, and chunk AGx counts the disagreements).
- `no_channel`: AG2 has no channel for the mark.
- `no_condition`: no condition exists for the cell, so the track is not queryable. No scRNA-seq conditioning is used (PI ruling 36); the track is reported as not queryable.
The audit is at the level of condition and channel, not of accession, because AG2 pools replicates and publishes no accession table. A track is "queryable" when its status is one of the first four. The audit also writes, per cell, the count of observed AG2 channels (the information AG2 had about the cell) beside the count of miniCANDI input tracks of the eval set.
Comparison sets (names of this amendment; PI ruling 34):
- The "unseen-by-both set" of a model: tracks with status `unseen` that the model never trained on. For `final` it holds validation and blind tracks; for `final_tv` and the published EIC entries it holds blind tracks only. The headline contrast against AG2 is computed on this set.
- The "AG2-trained set" of a model: tracks with status `observed_matched` or `observed_other_read_type` that the model never trained on. This is the second, required comparison of ruling 34. Every row and every contrast on it carries the label `ag2_trained_on: AG2 trained on these tracks, the count model did not`. It is never the headline and enters no rank. The PI expects AG2 to win it by a large margin; the pre-registration states that expectation.
- `sibling_condition`, `no_channel` and `no_condition` tracks are listed with their count and scored for no AG2 row.
Expected counts: not computable from files now (no AG2 metadata is on disk). The candidates are 45 validation and 51 blind tracks; the fact above predicts few or no unseen blind tracks, and so a large AG2-trained set on the blind split. The counts come from: `$RC main tools/onion_data/ag2_audit.py run --ledger $O/eic/accession_ledger.tsv --experiments $O/catalog/encode/experiments.tsv --observed $AGD/meta/ag2_observed.tsv --conditions $AGD/meta/ag2_conditions.tsv --channels $AGD/meta/ag2_channels.tsv --out $AGD/audit` (prints one line per role and status). Minimum for a contrast: 10 tracks from at least 3 cells in the unseen-by-both set of a split; below it the table prints the rows and the words `too few tracks for a contrast`, for either set. If the headline set is under the minimum, the report says so and cites Max's Benchmark F1 (178 targets) as the powered test; no non-EIC track is added here (D8, D16). The same shortage is the reason for ruling 34b.

**B.2 The map into AG2's space.** Max's space is primary. AG2's own space is secondary.
- Primary space (`signal_arcsinh`, 25 bp). Truth: asinh of `signal.h5`. miniCANDI and the NB baselines: asinh of the D9b expectation signal of the predicted NB (the existing `sig_` arrays of `score.py`). Published EIC entries: asinh of the submitted track (chunk XE). AG2 ChIP-seq: the raw fold change of the matched channel, 128 bp to 25 bp by the overlap-weighted mean, no transform. AG2 DNase-seq and ATAC-seq: the 1 bp normalized insertion signal summed to 25 bp, no transform. Then every row gets the same oracle isotonic recalibration to the truth, per track (2,000 quantile bins). `rspec_cal` and `pearson_cal` are computed after it; Spearman and the peak AUPRC do not need it. `rspec_cal_128bp` is the same metric after the truth and every row are averaged to AG2's own 128 bp bins (at 25 bp AG2 is piecewise constant).
- Secondary space A, ChIP-seq fold change over control (AG2's own unit). For a predicted NB mean `mu` per 25 bp bin: `FE = pileup_from_counts(mu, d, rl) / lambda`, with `d` = `frag_len`, `rl` = `read_length` of the target row, and `lambda = macs2_lambda(lambda_bg = d * N_treat / gsize, control_pileup = pileup_from_counts(control, d, control_rl), ratio_treat2control = N_treat / N_control)`: exactly the lambda of the D9b ChIP-seq route (`signal_track`, lines 290-302), from the cell's observed control, or from the predicted control for Caco-2 and SJSA1, with the same `lambda_source` label. No pseudocount. The fold change is linear in the count, so its NB expectation equals the value at `mu`: expectation and plug-in are one track, and `n` is not used. The truth in this space is the same function applied to the observed counts of the target. The read type enters only through the target's own covariates; the choice between AG2's single-end and paired-end channel is the "matched" rule of section A. Scored at 25 bp (AG2 by the overlap mean), with the uncalibrated Pearson (the paper's measure) and Spearman. It is secondary because Max's decision makes the recalibrated signal space primary; the reports say so where they show it.
- Secondary space B, accessibility. AG2's unit is insertions normalized to a genome-wide sum of 1e8. For DNase-seq and ATAC-seq: `value per 25 bp bin = mu * 25 / (rl + 24) * 1e8 / depth_reads` (the tags behind a binned count, `signal_from_counts.py` "Counts to the caller's units", over the track's total tags). Limit, stated in every table: our counts spread a read over its whole span, AG2 counts its 5' end, so the two differ by a blur of about one read length; the comparison is therefore made at 25 bp and never below.
- Not done: AG2 in count space (decision 7); a log or pseudocount variant (moot under recalibration, as Max notes).

**B.3 Peaks.**
- Truth peaks, primary: the ENCODE peak file of the track's own experiment (narrowPeak, GRCh38, the file ENCODE marks as the default of the experiment's newest analysis; replicated peaks before pseudo-replicated peaks; the chosen accession and md5 are recorded per track). This is the paper's source and Max's. Reason: our uniform pipeline calls no peaks (`fastq_uniform.py:11`).
- Limit of the two truths, stated in every report: the ENCODE peak files come from ENCODE's processing, which also made AG2's training fold-change tracks; the primary signal truth comes from our reprocessing, which made the count model's training counts. Each truth favors one side, so both peak sources are shown.
- Truth peaks, secondary ("pipeline-derived"): bins of our own truth signal with -log10 p >= 2, i.e. p <= 0.01, the p-value threshold of the ENCODE histone pipeline's MACS2 call. Reported beside the primary; it needs no download and exists for every track with `signal.h5`.
- Tiles (Max): 500 bp tiles of 20 bins inside the eval regions. Consensus tile: any track of the assay in the tile set has a peak bin in it. Positive: the target has one. Negatives: the other consensus tiles; background is no negative (the paper's rule). The tile set of a validation score holds the training and validation tracks of the assay; the tile set of the blind score also holds the blind tracks and is built only inside the blind pass, after a ledger `started` row (the blind peak files are fetched there, not before).
- Ranking score of a row: the mean of the row's primary-space prediction over the tile, before recalibration (Max). AUPRC by `candi.bench.binary.average_precision`. Reported: the unweighted mean over tracks (Max), the mean weighted by each track's positive fraction (the paper), and 8 peak-frequency bins of 12.5% of the assay's tracks (an assay with fewer than 8 tracks in the tile set prints only the bins that exist).
- Binary peaks from predicted counts (the PI's request). For a count model the NB gives a peak probability per bin: `P_NB(n, mu)[ -log10 P(Poisson(lambda) > floor(pileup(k))) >= 2 ]`, the same score and lambda as D9b. Tile probability = the largest bin probability of the tile. Two uses: a second ranking score (`auprc_peakprob`), and a binary call at probability 0.5 (`nbpeak_precision`, `nbpeak_recall`, `nbpeak_f1`). AG2 and the published entries give no probability, and these columns print `not applicable` for them. The binary call that is compared across methods is the same for every row: a tile is called when any of its bins has a recalibrated value >= asinh(2), i.e. the truth's own p <= 0.01 threshold after the oracle recalibration (`peak_precision`, `peak_recall`, `peak_f1`, labelled `oracle threshold`).

**B.4 Regions and API cost.** One call = one 1 Mb window (1,048,576 bp) for one condition; a call returns all 43 histone channels. Whether one call may also return the DNase-seq and ATAC-seq outputs is checked by chunk AG0; if not, a cell with an unseen accessibility track costs a second call per window.
- Eval regions (ours, the comparison). One window centred on each region (a region is 409,600 bp; the margin is 319,488 bp on each side). 256 calls per condition. Conditions: the cells with a validation or blind track; 26 + 12 cells with 2 cells in both roles (ledger), so at most 36. Calls: 36 x 256 = 9,216. If the blind eval set of chunk E8a has other regions, the 12 blind cells cost 12 x 256 = 3,072 more. If accessibility needs its own call: at most 9,216 more. Planned 9,216; worst case 9,216 + 3,072 + 9,216 = 21,504.
- chr20 to chr22 (Max's). ceil(64,444,167 / 524,288) + ceil(46,709,983 / 524,288) + ceil(50,818,468 / 524,288) = 123 + 90 + 97 = 310 windows per condition at his stride (his measured figure: 23,946 calls / 78 conditions = 307). This run makes 0 such calls: the gate reads his cache.
- Genome-wide, which a row on the EIC challenge board would need (chr1 to chr22 and chrX at the 524,288 bp stride): 5,791 windows per condition; 12 blind cells x 5,791 = 69,492 calls (46,368 at a 786,432 bp stride). Not made: PI ruling 35 gives AG2 no row on the challenge board.
- Against the observed quota: `RESOURCE_EXHAUSTED` came after 288 to about 960 calls in Max's fetches, and his whole fetch ran at 23,946 / (89 x 60) = 4.48 calls per second, quota waits included. 9,216 calls are therefore about 9,216 / 4.48 = 34 minutes including waits, at his measured rate. The fetch runs on a Nibi login node in invocations of at most 2,000 calls (about 7.4 minutes each at that rate). One invocation can last longer: the 8 backoff waits sum to 30 + 60 + 120 + 240 + 480 + 600 + 600 + 600 = 2,730 s before exit 75. So each invocation runs under `nohup` with its log in `$AGD/logs/`, and a dropped session is safe because every finished chunk of 25 windows is already in the cache.

**B.5 Leaderboard rows (PI rulings 35 and 37).** One new table, `ag2_board`, on our evaluation regions, in two files: `ag2_board_val.tsv` (validation tracks) and `ag2_board_blind.tsv` (blind tracks, written only by the blind pass). One row per (method, table, track set, assay class or `all`). Methods: `ag2_matched`, `ag2_otherend`; `minicandi_final` (mean of 3 seeds, with max minus min) and seed 0; `minicandi_final_tv` (blind only); `training_mean_signal` (Max's training average); `position_average` and `marginal_nb` (the D14 baselines through the D9b route); `closest5_oracle`; `retrieval` (validation only: its tool serves no blind split, and the blind board prints `not available`); `pseudoreplicate_half`; on blind tracks the 25 published EIC entries. Tables: `headline` (`leave_one_assay_out`, observed control) and `control_free` (`accessibility_only`, predicted control; validation only). Track sets: `unseen_by_both` (the headline of ruling 34) and `ag2_trained` (the second comparison of ruling 34). Columns: `method, table, track_set, comparison_label, assay_class, n_tracks, n_cells, rspec_cal, pearson_cal, spearman, auprc, auprc_weighted, auprc_f1 .. auprc_f8, auprc_peakprob, peak_precision, peak_recall, peak_f1, nbpeak_precision, nbpeak_recall, nbpeak_f1, rspec, pearson, rspec_cal_128bp, fe_pearson_25bp, fe_spearman_25bp` and, for each of the first four metrics, `<name>_ci_lo, <name>_ci_hi`; then the fairness labels `ag2_status` (the track set's statuses with counts), `ag2_api_label` (`observed`, `imputed_supported`, `imputed_exploratory` with counts), `trained_on_track` (per method: `no`, `yes`, or `yes: n of N`), `trained_on_cell_other_tracks` (`yes` for every method here), `reads_target_cell_inputs_at_test_positions` (`yes` for count models and the retrieval and oracle rows, `no` for AG2), `uses_target_cell_control` (`yes` or `no`, decision 11), `truth_peaks` (`encode` or `pipeline_derived`), `recalibration` (`oracle_isotonic_2000`), `noise_floor_row` (`pseudoreplicate_half`, half against half). `comparison_label` is `headline: neither trained on these tracks` or `ag2_trained_on: AG2 trained on these tracks, the count model did not`. A second file per split, `ag2_contrasts_<split>.tsv`, holds per-target differences against `ag2_matched` with the bootstrap interval and the count of targets ahead, for both track sets, each row with its `track_set` and `comparison_label`; the two sets are never pooled. `ag2_board_blind.tsv` is cited by chunk XCb's `EIC_COMPARISON.md` under the published field, so AG2 stands beside miniCANDI, the baselines and the external entries in one place. AG2 has no row on the challenge board `final_board.tsv` (ruling 35). Headline metrics 3 to 7 print `not applicable` on AG2 rows (decision 7).

**B.6 What is reused from Max, and the reproduction gate.** Reused: the fetch logic of S11 `scripts/api/fetch_ag2.py` and `pack_ag2.py` (ported, with the source lines named in the docstring); `to25`, the isotonic recalibration, `tile_calls` and the bootstrap of S12 `score_f1.py` and `summarize_f1.py` (ported, not rewritten); the condition-mapping rule of `make_split_f1.py`. Read only, for the gate: `$MAXP/ag2pred/<chrom>/<biosample>__<assay>.npz`; his target table `output/api/targets_ag2.tsv`; his per-target scores (`$MAXP/scores/f1_c20_22/` when it exists, else `/project/def-maxwl/maxwl/epi_imputation/s11/ag2/same_c20_22`); his peak tiles `/scratch/maxwl/epi_imputation/s11/ag2/tiles/`; the truth he used (`CANDI_STORE/merged`, layer `pval`). Gate (chunk AGm): on a seeded pick of 12 of his targets that covers at least 4 marks, over chr20 to chr22, our functions score his cached AG2 matched channel against his truth, and the result is compared with his per-target numbers. Tolerance, maximum absolute difference over the 12: `pearson_cal` 0.005, Spearman 0.005, peak AUPRC 0.005, `rspec_cal` 0.01 (our `metrics.r_spec` against his `metrics_lib`). Pass prints `gate: PASS`. A failure is reported with the metric and the cause; nothing is tuned to fit, and the foreman calls the planner. The gate makes no API call and reads no EIC track.

**Pinned interface F: method track files (written by AGd, AGb, AGf `pack`; read by AGc).** A directory `<dir>/<regime>/` with `tracks.json` = `{"method", "kind": "run"|"baseline"|"external"|"ag2"|"oracle_baseline"|"replicate_floor", "evalset_sha256", "regions_sha256" (sha256 of the JSON of the region list and `region_bins`), "split", "regime", "region_bins", "n_regions", "lambda_source", "targets": [measurement_id]}` and, per target, float32 arrays of shape `[n_regions * region_bins]` in the eval set's region order: `<measurement_id>.signal.npy` (the primary-space prediction before recalibration); `<measurement_id>.signal_pc.npy` (the same with the predicted control as lambda source; run and baseline kinds, only when the scorer ran with `--lambda-source predicted_control`); `<measurement_id>.truth.npy` (run and baseline kinds; also the `pseudoreplicate_half` directory, whose truth is its own other half); `<measurement_id>.native.npy` and `<measurement_id>.native_truth.npy` (secondary space A or B; run, baseline and `ag2` kinds; `native_truth` from run and baseline kinds only); `<measurement_id>.peakprob.npy` (run and NB baseline kinds only). An `ag2` directory holds `<measurement_id>.matched.signal.npy` and `<measurement_id>.otherend.signal.npy` in place of `signal.npy` (`native` is the same array for AG2), plus `channels.tsv` (`measurement_id, condition, matched_channel, otherend_channel, api_label_matched, api_label_otherend`) and the empty file `EVALUATION_ONLY`.

**Pinned interface G: normalized AG2 metadata (written by AG0; read by AGl, AGa, AGf).** `ag2_observed.tsv`: `output_type` (`CHIP_HISTONE`, `DNASE`, `ATAC`), `condition` (the string that a prediction call takes), `biosample_curie`, `biosample_name`, `target` (the mark; empty for accessibility), `endedness` (`single`, `paired`), `channel`, `channel_index`, `data_source`, `n_observed_tracks`; one row per observed (condition, channel), human only. `ag2_channels.tsv`: `output_type, channel_index, channel, target, endedness`. `ag2_conditions.tsv`: `condition, biosample_curies` (`;` joined), `biosample_name, n_observed_encode_bulk` (the count of "ENCODE bulk tracks" as section B.1 defines them), `has_observed_accessibility (0|1)`. `META_SCHEMA.md` maps each column to the API's own column name.

**Pinned interface H: `tools/onion_eval/ag2_space.py` (AGs).** `resample_mean(x, src_start_bp, src_bin_bp, dst_start_bp, dst_bin_bp, n_dst) -> float64[n_dst]` (overlap-weighted mean; 128 to 25 bp and 25 to 128 bp) and `resample_sum` (same arguments; 1 bp to 25 bp). `fold_change_track(mu, control_counts, covariates, *, gsize=MACS2_GSIZE_HS) -> (float32, lambda_source)` with the covariate keys of the ChIP-seq route of `signal_track`. `normalized_insertions(mu, covariates) -> float32`. `native_from_nb(n, mu, row, *, ds, regions, rb, predicted_control=None) -> (float32, lambda_source)`, the signature of `signal_from_nb`; it dispatches by `route_of`. `peak_probability_from_nb(n, mu, row, *, ds, regions, rb, predicted_control=None, threshold=2.0, dnase_route=DNASE_ROUTE_DEFAULT) -> float32`. `isotonic_recalibrate(pred, truth, n_bins=2000, valid=None) -> float64`. Numpy and scipy only; no import of `alphagenome`.

#### AGf Fetch tool with a restartable cache and quota backoff   wave Y1   task: new
Goal: Write the tool that fetches AG2 predictions for a list of conditions on a set of windows, survives the quota, and packs the result as method track files.
Owns: `tools/onion_data/ag2_fetch.py`, `tests/test_onion_data_ag2_fetch.py`.
Interfaces: ported from `$MAXR` S11 `scripts/api/fetch_ag2.py` and `pack_ag2.py` (name file and lines in the docstring). Module-level imports: the standard library and numpy only (the client venv has no `candi`, torch or h5py); `alphagenome` is imported inside `_client()`. `ag2_fetch.py fetch --conditions <tsv: condition> (--evalset <json> | --chroms chr20,chr21,chr22) --outputs chip_histone[,dnase,atac] --cache <dir> [--model all_folds] [--max-calls 2000] [--workers 5] [--chunk 25]`. Windows: with `--evalset`, one window of 1,048,576 bp centred on each region, shifted inward at a chromosome end; with `--chroms`, Max's tiling (stride 524,288 bp, each bin from the nearest window centre). Cache: `<cache>/<model>/<condition slug>/part_<first window index>.npz`, one file per chunk of 25 windows, written to a temporary name and renamed; it holds, per window, the ChIP array at 128 bp for all channels cut to the region plus 32 bins on each side (float16, clipped at 6e4), the accessibility arrays summed to the 25 bp grid (float32), the window coordinates, the channel names and the `imputation_mode` label per channel. A rerun fetches only missing windows. On `RESOURCE_EXHAUSTED`: wait 30 s x 2^k, at most 600 s, 8 tries; after the 8th the tool exits 75 with a consistent cache. It exits 0 when no window is missing. `<cache>/calls.tsv` appends `time, condition, window, status, seconds` (status = the code name only; no key, no project number). `ag2_fetch.py status --conditions ... --evalset ... --cache ...` prints `windows: N, cached: M, missing: N - M` per condition. `ag2_fetch.py pack --cache <dir> --evalset <json> --audit <ag2_eic_audit.tsv> --tables <dir> --split val|blind --out <dir>` writes an `ag2` directory of pinned interface F for the targets of that split whose status is queryable (matched and other read type by the rule of section A; ChIP by `resample_mean` re-stated in numpy here, held equal to `ag2_space.resample_mean` by a test that skips if that module is absent); `pack` reads `measurements.tsv` as text and no count file. Tests, with a fake client: a kill between two chunks loses no finished chunk; a rerun makes calls only for missing windows; 3 quota errors then success gives 3 waits (the sleep function is injected); 8 errors give exit 75; `--max-calls` stops cleanly; the key never appears in `calls.tsv`, the cache or the captured output; a key file of mode 644 is refused, and so is one with an empty line or two lines; the module imports with `alphagenome` absent.

#### AGb Signal-space baselines of Max: training mean and closest-5 oracle   wave Y1   task: new
Goal: Write the two baselines of Max's design, and the replicate-half reference row, as method track files.
Owns: `tools/onion_eval/ag2_baselines.py`, `tests/test_onion_eval_ag2_baselines.py`.
Interfaces: `ag2_baselines.py run --tables <dir> --evalset <json> --truth-from <method track dir> --split val|blind [--allow-blind] --out <dir>` writes three directories of pinned interface F, kind `oracle_baseline`: `pseudoreplicate_half` (kind `replicate_floor`: `signal` = asinh of the target's `pr1_signal.h5`, and its own `truth` = asinh of the target's `pr2_signal.h5`; a target without halves is left out and counted), `training_mean_signal` (per target, the mean over the training tracks of the assay, other cells only, of their signal) and `closest5_oracle` (per block, the mean of the 5 such tracks most correlated with the truth of the block; a region of 16,384 bins is two blocks, of 8,000 and 8,384 bins, so the 384-bin tail joins the second block). A training track has no `signal.h5` (`build_list.py:54`), so its signal is `signal_from_counts.signal_track(observed counts, mode="plug_in")` with its own observed control and covariates, then asinh; a training track with no control is left out and counted. The truth comes from `--truth-from` (a run or baseline directory of AGd), never from a blind file read by this tool, and the two halves of `pseudoreplicate_half` are the only target files it reads. `--split blind` needs `--allow-blind`; the tool then appends a `started` row before its first read and a `scored` row at the end, with `score.ledger_append`, to `BLIND_PASSES.tsv` beside the eval set under the key `oracle_baselines:<resolved out dir>` (the key holds no `ag2:`), and refuses a second blind run without `--repeat-blind-reason`. Tests on the synthetic tables of `tools/minicandi/synth.py`: the mean of identical tracks is that track; the half row equals asinh of the planted half and its truth is the other half; the oracle picks a planted copy of the truth; the target's own cell is never a candidate; a blind split without the flag raises; a blind run writes exactly two ledger rows under its key.
Depends on: none
Test: `PYTHONPATH=$PWD/src /Users/mforooz/miniforge3/envs/candii/bin/python -m pytest tests/test_onion_eval_ag2_baselines.py -q` prints 0 failed.

#### AGa Run the leakage audit on the EIC ledger   wave Y2   task: new
Goal: State which EIC tracks AG2 trained on, which are queryable, and the size of each fair comparison set; write the contrast down before any score exists.
Owns: `results/<T:AGa>/` (`AG2_LEAKAGE.md`, `ag2_eic_audit.tsv`, `ag2_fair_sets.tsv`, `fetch_conditions.tsv`, `PREREGISTRATION.md`, `runall.sh`, `MANIFEST.tsv`); Nibi `$AGD/audit/`.
Interfaces: `bash tools/onion_kit/remote_check.sh --cluster nibi main tools/onion_data/ag2_audit.py run --ledger /project/def-maxwl/mforooz/ONION/eic/accession_ledger.tsv --experiments /project/def-maxwl/mforooz/ONION/catalog/encode/experiments.tsv --observed /project/def-maxwl/mforooz/ONION/ag2/meta/ag2_observed.tsv --conditions /project/def-maxwl/mforooz/ONION/ag2/meta/ag2_conditions.tsv --channels /project/def-maxwl/mforooz/ONION/ag2/meta/ag2_channels.tsv --out /project/def-maxwl/mforooz/ONION/ag2/audit`; the three output tables come to the laptop (each under 1 MB). `AG2_LEAKAGE.md`: counts per role and status; the blind tracks by status, by name; the cells that are not queryable; the sizes of `unseen_by_both` and of `ag2_trained` per split and model against the minimum of 10 tracks from 3 cells; a cross-check of the planning fact (which of the 12 blind cells are among the paper's 32 held-out conditions, by ontology term where AG0's tables give it); the tracks whose experiment the Flashzoi table (`cruxvault/results/flashzoi_overlap/overlap_tracks.tsv`) also lists; the three limits in plain words (condition-and-channel level, not accession level; replicates pooled; the API model is not the paper's benchmark model). `PREREGISTRATION.md`, written before any AG2 score: the primary contrast (`minicandi_final`, mean of 3 seeds, `leave_one_assay_out`, minus `ag2_matched`, `rspec_cal`, on `unseen_by_both`, validation and blind apart), the second, required comparison of ruling 34 (the same contrast on `ag2_trained`, labelled, with the PI's expectation that AG2 wins by a large margin), the secondaries (the `control_free` table; `rspec_cal_128bp`; `pearson_cal`; Spearman; AUPRC; per assay class; `final_tv` on blind), the call rule (interval excludes 0), and the expectation in a sentence (Max's sprint 11 found AG2 ahead of the full CANDI by 0.07 to 0.12 in `rspec_cal` on 33 tracks). The slip carries one finding text and the notepad entry, and the measured blind counts per track set.
- Gate GA (the foreman, after AGa and before AGx). The foreman relays `AG2_LEAKAGE.md` to the PI in one paragraph: the size of each comparison set per split. (1) If no validation or blind track is queryable, AGx to AGz do not run and the foreman reports that. (2) If `unseen_by_both` is under the minimum in both splits, the fetch still runs, because ruling 34 requires the `ag2_trained` comparison; the foreman tells the PI that the headline set is too small on EIC and that ruling 34b carries the fair test to the extended dataset. (3) Otherwise AGx starts.
Depends on: AG0 (the metadata on Nibi), AGl (merged and pushed as kit `main`)
Compute: under 1 CPU-h. Wall: 2 h.
Test: `awk -F'\t' 'NR>1' cruxvault/results/<T:AGa>/ag2_eic_audit.tsv | wc -l` prints 363, `awk -F'\t' 'NR>1 && $2=="blind"' cruxvault/results/<T:AGa>/ag2_eic_audit.tsv | wc -l` prints 51, and `test -s cruxvault/results/<T:AGa>/PREREGISTRATION.md`.

#### AGd Dump hook: method track files from the scorer   wave Y2   task: new
Goal: Let the scorer write the per-bin truth and prediction that it already holds, so that every method reaches the AG2 scorer through one file format.
Owns: one additive edit in `tools/onion_eval/score.py`, one pass-through option in `tools/onion_eval/external_signal.py` and one in `tools/onion_eval/baseline_retrieval.py`; `tests/test_onion_eval_ag2_dump.py`.
Interfaces: the hook sits on `Scorer` (constructor argument `dump_signal=<dir>`, `dump_targets=<set or None>`), so that `score.score(..., dump_signal=...)`, `score.py --dump-signal <dir> [--dump-targets <file of measurement ids>]`, `external_signal.py score --dump-signal ...` and `baseline_retrieval.py run --dump-signal ...` (its `RetrievalScorer` is a `Scorer`; validation only) all reach it. For each scored imputation target of each scored regime, `Scorer._signal_target` writes, under `<dir>/<regime>/`, the arrays of pinned interface F: `signal` and `truth` from the expectation arrays it already computes (`sig_pred`, `sig_truth`); `signal_pc` from the `sig_pc_` arrays when the scorer runs with `--lambda-source predicted_control` (for a baseline the scorer's own substitute, the training-average control; a predictor with no such control writes no `signal_pc` and `tracks.json` says why); and, for a run or an NB baseline, `native`, `native_truth` (`ag2_space.native_from_nb` on the prediction and on the observed counts) and `peakprob` (`ag2_space.peak_probability_from_nb`). No mask array is written (decision 6). An external entry writes `signal` only. `kind`, `method`, `lambda_source` and `regions_sha256` in `tracks.json` come from the scorer. Merge rule: without the option, `scores.json`, `suffstats.npz` and the ledger rows are byte-identical to the base commit (a digest pinned in the test on the synthetic fixture, per platform as chunk OXl did). With the option, `scores.json` is byte-identical to a run without it at the same `--lambda-source`. The option changes no blind rule: a blind dump happens only inside a blind pass that the ledger already records.
Depends on: AGs (merged)
Test: `bash tools/onion_kit/push_kit.sh AGd && bash tools/onion_kit/remote_check.sh AGd -m pytest tests/test_onion_eval_ag2_dump.py tests/test_onion_eval_score.py tests/test_onion_eval_external_signal.py tests/test_onion_eval_baseline_retrieval.py -q` prints 0 failed.

#### AGc The same-track scorer and the `ag2_board` table   wave Y2   task: new
Goal: Score AG2 and every other method on the same tracks with Max's metrics, with the fairness labels, and write the board and the contrasts.
Owns: `tools/onion_eval/ag2_score.py`, `tests/test_onion_eval_ag2_score.py`.
Interfaces: `ag2_score.py score --evalset <json> --audit <ag2_eic_audit.tsv> --tables <dir> --truth-from <run or baseline method track dir> --method <name>=<method track dir> [...] --ag2 <ag2 pack dir> --peaks <dir of narrowPeak> --split val|blind [--allow-blind] [--repeat-blind-reason "<text>"] [--table headline|control_free] [--n-boot 2000] --out <dir>` writes `<out>/per_target.tsv` (one row per method and target with every metric of section B.5 and the target's `status`, `api_label`, `trained_on_track`), `ag2_board_<split>.tsv`, `ag2_contrasts_<split>.tsv` and `SCORED`. Metrics: recalibration by `ag2_space.isotonic_recalibrate`; `rspec_cal`, `rspec` and `rspec_cal_128bp` by `metrics.r_spec` on all bins of the regions (decision 6) with `a` = the `training_mean_signal` track of the target (required among the methods); `--table headline` reads `leave_one_assay_out/<id>.signal.npy`, `--table control_free` reads `accessibility_only/<id>.signal_pc.npy` and prints `not available` for a method without it; the `pseudoreplicate_half` row is scored against its own `truth` (half against half) and is exempt from the equal-truth check; Pearson; Spearman on Max's fixed subsample (2,000,000 bins, seed 0) when the track is longer; peaks by `ag2_peaks`; the fold-change columns from the `native` arrays at 25 bp; the binary call of decision 17 for every row, and the NB-probability columns for rows that have `peakprob`. Rows are aggregated over the two track sets of section B.1. Contrasts are computed on both sets, never pooled, each with its `comparison_label` (ruling 34), with the cluster bootstrap over cells (ported from `$MAXR` S12 `summarize_f1.py`); a set under 10 tracks or 3 cells prints `too few tracks for a contrast` in place of an interval. `trained_on_track` per method comes from the method's `kind` and the target's role: a run trained on `train` (or on `train,val` when `tracks.json` of its run says so); external entries on `train,val`; AG2 from the audit. It refuses: a method directory whose `regions_sha256` or `region_bins` differs from the eval set's, or that lacks a scored target (the file hash `evalset_sha256` is recorded per method and not compared, because `final_tv` is scored on `evalset_blind_tv.json` and `final` on `evalset_blind.json`: same regions and targets, other inputs); two truth arrays of one target that differ (the `replicate_floor` kind apart); a target listed in two track sets. `--split blind` needs `--allow-blind` and writes `started` and `scored` rows with `score.ledger_append` to `BLIND_PASSES.tsv` beside the eval set under the key `ag2:<resolved ag2 pack dir>`, and refuses a second pass without `--repeat-blind-reason`. `ag2_score.py gate --targets <tsv> --pred <Max's ag2pred dir> --store <CANDI_STORE dir> --tiles <dir> --reference <Max's per-target tsv> --out <dir>` is the reproduction gate of section B.6: it reads Max's packed arrays and the store's `pval` layer through `candi.store.reader`, scores with the same functions, and prints per metric the maximum absolute difference and `gate: PASS` or `gate: FAIL`. Tests on synthetic method track files: a planted monotone transform of the truth scores `rspec_cal` and `pearson_cal` of 1 while its raw Pearson is below 1; an `ag2_trained` track enters only the labelled contrast and never the headline one; two method directories with different file hashes and equal regions are accepted, and one with other regions is refused; the half row is scored against its own truth; the labels appear on every row; a second blind pass is refused; the bootstrap interval of a planted constant difference excludes 0.
Depends on: AGs, AGk (merged); pinned interface F
Test: `PYTHONPATH=$PWD/src /Users/mforooz/miniforge3/envs/candii/bin/python -m pytest tests/test_onion_eval_ag2_score.py -q` prints 0 failed.

#### AGm Reproduction gate against Max's numbers   wave Y3   task: new
Goal: Show that our scorer gives Max's numbers on his cached AG2 predictions, before it scores any EIC track.
Owns: `results/<T:AGm>/` (`MAX_REPRO.md`, `max_repro.tsv`, `inputs_found.md`, `runall.sh`, `MANIFEST.tsv`).
Interfaces: step 1, existence, read only: `hpc run fir 'ls /project/def-maxwl/maxwl/epi_imputation/s12/fair/ag2pred | head; ls /project/def-maxwl/maxwl/epi_imputation/s12/fair/scores 2>/dev/null; ls /project/def-maxwl/maxwl/epi_imputation/s11/ag2 /scratch/maxwl/epi_imputation/s11/ag2/tiles | head'`; `inputs_found.md` records what exists, the layout of one `.npz` (keys, shape, dtype), and which score table serves as the reference (S12's `f1_c20_22_targets.tsv` if present, else S11's `same_c20_22`, with its predictions under `/scratch/maxwl/epi_imputation/s11/ag2/ag2pred/`). Max's small target tables are read from `$MAXR` with `gh` (no secret in any command). Step 2: pick 12 targets with a seeded draw that covers at least 4 marks; first `bash tools/onion_kit/push_kit.sh --cluster fir main` (the default cluster is Nibi), then `bash tools/onion_kit/remote_run.sh --cluster fir --name ag2_gate main 4 32000 04:00:00 tools/onion_eval/ag2_score.py gate --targets <tsv> --pred <dir> --store <the store path that his score script names> --tiles <dir> --reference <tsv> --out /scratch/mforooz/onion_ag2_gate` with the exclude list `.orchestrate/exclude_fir`. Nothing under Max's directories is written. Step 3: `MAX_REPRO.md` gives the 12 targets, our value and his per metric, the maximum absolute difference against the tolerance of section B.6, and `gate: PASS` or `gate: FAIL` with the cause. On `FAIL` the chunk changes no code; the foreman calls the planner. If Max's score tables do not exist yet, the chunk reports that and the foreman reruns it once they do.
Depends on: AGc, AGs, AGk (merged and pushed as kit `main`)
Compute: about 4 CPU-h on Fir. Wall: 3 h.
Test: `grep -c '^gate: PASS' cruxvault/results/<T:AGm>/MAX_REPRO.md` prints 1.

#### AGx Fetch AG2 for the EIC cells on the eval regions   wave 7   task: new   (waits for the frozen `$EV`)
Goal: Fetch and pack the AG2 predictions that the comparison needs, and nothing more.
Owns: `results/<T:AGx>/` (`FETCH.md`, `calls_summary.tsv`); Nibi `$AGD/cache/`, `$AGD/pack/eic_v1/`, `$AGD/peaks/`.
Interfaces: start when `$EV` is frozen (chunk E7a), gate AGm printed `PASS` and gate GA let it start. (1) Conditions: `fetch_conditions.tsv` of AGa (the conditions with a queryable validation or blind track; both comparison sets of ruling 34 need them). (2) Three calls first, then `status`. (3) Fetch in invocations of at most 2,000 calls, from a login node, one process: `hpc run nibi 'module load gcc arrow/25.0.0 python/3.11 && source /project/def-maxwl/mforooz/ONION/envs/ag2/bin/activate && nohup nice python /project/def-maxwl/mforooz/onion_kit/main/tools/onion_data/ag2_fetch.py fetch --conditions /project/def-maxwl/mforooz/ONION/ag2/audit/fetch_conditions.tsv --evalset /project/def-maxwl/mforooz/ONION/eval/eic_v1/evalset.json --outputs chip_histone --cache /project/def-maxwl/mforooz/ONION/ag2/cache --max-calls 2000 > /project/def-maxwl/mforooz/ONION/ag2/logs/fetch_$(date +%s).log 2>&1 &'` (one invocation at a time; a dropped session is safe), repeated until `status` prints `missing: 0`; exit 75 means wait and rerun, never a new cache. `--outputs` gains `dnase,atac` only for conditions with `needs_accessibility = 1`. Stop and report if the quota waits exceed 6 hours in total or if more than 21,504 calls would be needed. (4) `pack --split val` into `$AGD/pack/eic_v1/` through `$RC main` (it reads no count file). The blind pack is not written here. (5) The ENCODE peak files of the training and validation tracks: `ag2_peaks.py fetch --roles train,val --out $AGD/peaks` on the login node. `FETCH.md`: calls made, quota waits, seconds per call, the count of (condition, channel) labels from the API that disagree with the audit's `api_label` (expected 0; each one is listed), the cache size and file count (`du -sh`, `find | wc -l`), and the check that the key string is in no file: `hpc run nibi 'grep -rlF -f ~/.secrets/alphagenome_api_key /project/def-maxwl/mforooz/ONION/ag2 | wc -l'` prints 0 (the pattern is read from the file, so the key is on no command line).
Depends on: AGa, AGf, AGk (merged), AGm (gate passed), E7a
Compute: planned 9,216 API calls, about 34 minutes including quota waits at Max's measured rate; worst case 21,504 calls. Storage: 36 conditions x 256 windows x 3,264 bins x 43 channels x 2 bytes = 2.6 GB in about 36 x 11 = 396 files; peak files about 2 GB in 312 files. No sbatch job.

Interfaces: required artefacts: `$AGD/scores/eic_v2/blind/SCORED` and the blind method track directories that the addendum below makes BPa write. No job of this chunk reads a blind track: it reads the score tables. `ag2_ledger_check.txt`: `BLIND_PASSES.tsv` holds exactly one `started` and one `scored` row with the key `ag2:<pack dir>`, one `started` and one `scored` row with the key `oracle_baselines:<dir>`, one `started` and one `fetched` row with the key `peaks:<dir>`, and every method track directory that the AG2 score read belongs to a run of `FREEZE.md` or to a baseline or entry of BPa's list. `AG2_BLIND.md`: the blind board; the headline contrast for `final` and for `final_tv` on `unseen_by_both`, or the words `too few tracks for a contrast` with the counts; the second, required contrast of ruling 34 on `ag2_trained`, under its label and never as the headline, with the PI's stated expectation beside the result; validation beside blind; the label `uses_target_cell_control = yes` on the count-model rows (the blind pass has the headline table only). The slip carries the finding texts and the notepad entry.
- Addendum to the foreman's brief of chunk FDb: `FREEZE.md` also lists the AG2 jobs of the blind pass (the AG2 pack directory, the blind peak fetch, the blind baselines of AGb, the one `ag2_score.py score --split blind` job).
- Addendum to the foreman's brief of chunk BPa. Before submitting: `ag2_fetch.py fetch` with `--evalset $O/eval/eic_v2/evalset_blind.json` for the conditions of the blind tracks (cache hits if the regions equal those of `$EV`; at most 3,072 new calls), and `ag2_fetch.py pack --split blind --out $AGD/pack/eic_v2`. Fetching and packing AG2 read no blind truth. The blind peak files are not fetched here. In step (1), each blind `score.sh` line of `final`, `final_tv` and the baselines `position_average` and `marginal_nb` gets `--dump-signal $AGD/tracks/eic_v2/<name>[/seed<s>]`; in step (4), each `external_signal.sh` line gets `--dump-signal $AGD/tracks/eic_v2/entries/<team id>`. These flags add no pass and no ledger row. The lambda source of every blind line stays as `FREEZE.md` has it. New step (5), chained after steps (1) and (4), three jobs in this order, each once: `ag2_peaks.py fetch --roles blind --allow-blind --evalset $O/eval/eic_v2/evalset_blind.json --out $AGD/peaks` (from the login node; it writes its own ledger rows before the first blind download); `ag2_baselines.py run --split blind --allow-blind --truth-from $AGD/tracks/eic_v2/position_average ...` (key `oracle_baselines:`); then `ag2_score.py score --split blind --allow-blind --table headline --evalset $O/eval/eic_v2/evalset_blind.json --tables $T2 ... --out $AGD/scores/eic_v2/blind` (key `ag2:`), with `final`, `final_tv` and `final_tv_avg` as methods: the tool accepts their two eval-set files because their regions and targets are equal (chunk AGc), and it labels `final_tv` rows `inputs: training and validation tracks`. A crash is handled by the foreman-only repeat rule of BPa.
- Addendum to the foreman's brief of chunk XCb: `EIC_COMPARISON.md` cites `results/<T:AGz>/ag2_board_blind.tsv` under the published field, with the track status of every AG2 row, and states that AG2 has no row on `final_board.tsv` (PI ruling 35).
- Addendum to the foreman's briefs of RD and RSy: AG2 gets one slide and one paragraph: the same-track board with its labels, the headline contrast or the words that no fair contrast exists on EIC, the labelled second comparison of ruling 34, and Max's Benchmark F1 result cited as his. Benchmark results from API outputs may be shown (Terms of Service, read by the PI); raw AG2 tracks are not redistributed.
- Addendum to the foreman's brief of chunk G5x (PI ruling 34b; no compute now): `EXTENDED_GATE.md` gains a section "Fair test against AG2". Requirement: the test set of the extended dataset (the group split of D11) must hold enough tracks outside AG2's training set, i.e. with track status `unseen` and a queryable condition. Inputs: `results/<T:AGa>/ag2_eic_audit.tsv` and `$AGD/meta/` (the same three metadata tables), and `ag2_audit.py run` applied to the catalog's candidate experiments (`candidate_experiments.tsv`), which gives each candidate its track status at no API cost. The section states, per assay class, how many candidate tracks are `unseen` and queryable, and proposes the minimum for the split (at least 10 tracks from 3 cells per assay class, the minimum of section B.1; the planner fixes the figure when it writes the split after gate G5). The split chunk that the planner writes after gate G5 must check this requirement before it freezes the test set.
Depends on: BPa (jobs finished), AGwb
Compute: blind baselines 10 + scoring 5 = about 15 CPU-h inside BPa; this chunk about 1 CPU-h. Storage on `/scratch`: 25 entries x 51 targets x 16.8 MB = 21 GB, plus 7 run and baseline directories (3 seeds of `final`, `final_tv`, `final_tv_avg`, 2 baselines) x 5 arrays x 2 regimes x 51 x 16.8 MB = 60 GB; 81 GB in all. Wall: 3 h.
Test: `hpc run nibi 'grep -c "ag2:" /project/def-maxwl/mforooz/ONION/eval/eic_v2/BLIND_PASSES.tsv'` prints 2 (one `started`, one `scored`; the keys `oracle_baselines:` and `peaks:` hold no `ag2:`), and `test -s cruxvault/results/<T:AGz>/AG2_BLIND.md -a -s cruxvault/results/<T:AGz>/ag2_ledger_check.txt`.

**D. Acceptance, cost and risks of this amendment.**
- Acceptance criteria: four rows are appended to `## Acceptance criteria` (the unit tests without the client; the audit row counts and the pre-registration date; the reproduction gate; the blind board with its labels, both comparisons and its ledger rows).
- API calls: metadata and smoke at most 15; reproduction gate 0; eval regions planned 36 x 256 = 9,216, worst case 21,504; total planned about 9,230. No genome-wide fetch (ruling 35).
- Compute: AGm 4 (Fir) + AGa 1 + AGva 30 + AGvb 10 + AGwa 20 + AGwb 10 + blind 15 + AGz 1 = about 91 CPU-h, i.e. 0.3% of the 29,200 CPU-h of Amendment 2b. Storage: `$AGD` cache 2.6 GB and peaks 2 GB on `/project` (about 396 + 312 = 708 files); method track files at most 25 + 27 = 52 GB (validation) and 81 GB (blind) on `/scratch`, deleted by the foreman after VC.
- Critical path: unchanged. No chunk of the path D6b to FDb depends on a chunk of this amendment, and none of them reads an AG2 file (decision 16). BPa gains flags and three chained jobs. AGd edits `score.py` under the digest rule of Amendment 4, and the foreman does not push kit `main` between the start and the acceptance of D6f or of E7a.
- Risks, each with its check.
  - Quota. Check: `calls.tsv` and the exit code 75; invocations of 2,000 calls under `nohup`; the stop rule of AGx (6 hours of waits). The blog offers extra quota by a form due 2026-11-30; only the PI can file it.
  - Single-end and paired-end channel mismatch (all 51 blind tracks are paired-end; 43 of 45 validation tracks are single-end). Check: the `otherend` row beside `matched`; `channels.tsv` per target; a track observed only for the other read type is never counted as unseen.
  - EIC cells that AG2 cannot query. Check: `no_condition` and `no_channel` counts in `AG2_LEAKAGE.md`; such tracks are reported as not queryable and get no AG2 row (ruling 36).
  - AG2 trained on EIC blind tracks (expected, see the planning fact). Check: the audit; such tracks enter only the labelled second comparison of ruling 34, never the headline and never a rank; gate GA reports the sizes.
  - The count model's signal reads the target cell's control and AG2 does not. Check: the `uses_target_cell_control` label; the `control_free` table on validation.
  - 128 bp against 25 bp. Check: `rspec_cal_128bp`; `resample_mean` tests; the reproduction gate; Max's sprint 06 result that coarse prediction costs little at 25 bp is his, on his data, and is not assumed here.
  - Sum or mean per 128 bp bin, and the fold-change scale. Check: AG0's smoke; recalibrated metrics do not depend on the scale; the fold-change columns state the convention found.
  - The audit is at condition-and-channel level. A track called unseen could still share a donor or a replicate with a pooled AG2 track of another channel, and a track filed under a condition of another ontology term is not seen. Check: stated as a limit in every table; no accession-level claim is made.
  - Each truth favors one side (ENCODE peaks for AG2, our reprocessed signal for the count model). Check: both peak sources in every report.
  - Our scorer differs from Max's. Check: gate AGm, before any EIC score.
  - A blind look outside the pass. Check: `ag2_score.py`, `ag2_baselines.py` and the blind peak fetch refuse the blind split without the flag and each writes its own ledger rows before its first blind read; `ag2_ledger_check.txt`; fetching and packing AG2 read no truth.
  - AG2 numbers steer the ladder. Check: decision 16; `grep -rn "ONION/ag2" tools/onion_eval/select_rule.py tools/minicandi/` prints nothing (AGz's ledger check runs it).
  - AG2 outputs leak into training. Check: `$AGD` is outside every tables directory; `EVALUATION_ONLY`; no loader reads `$AGD` (`grep -rn "ag2" tools/minicandi/` prints nothing).
- Review minors declined. (1) "Cut secondary space B (accessibility units)": kept, because the PI task asks for the map into AG2's space per assay, and it is one function with one test. (2) "Drop the retrieval row": kept on validation through the hook on `Scorer`, because the PI wants that baseline in every table; it is absent on blind and the board says so.

**Decisions needed (Amendment 5).** None. The former lines (o) to (r) are answered by PI rulings 34 to 37 and by the PI's reading of the Terms of Service (2026-11-13).

### Amendment 6 (2026-11-13: freeze at a fixed time, then versioned updates)

Sixth planner call-back. Amended once in place after PI ruling 38 and the cold review `.orchestrate/review_A6.md` (2026-11-13): the three blocking findings are closed by the ruling (see "Review" at the end), and the interim tables `eic_v1_pre`, their guard and the early probes of the first draft are deleted. It changes no finished chunk, no earlier PI ruling, no decision D1 to D16, no Log line and no existing chunk section. Where it touches an existing chunk it says "addendum to the foreman's brief of chunk X". Every chunk below is `task: new`. Names as in Amendments 2 and 5. New names: `B=$O/eic/build_list.tsv`; `KF4=/project/def-maxwl/mforooz/onion_kit/KF4` (the build kit for every later submit); `RG=$O/eval/regions_v1/regions.json` (the regions file); `TS=$O/eic/tail/TAIL_STATUS.tsv` and `TD=$O/eic/tail/TAIL_DONE` (written by the cluster-side watchdog `slurm/onion_data/tail_watch.sh`, which the build-fix builder owns; it never submits and never deletes). "Freeze time" means 2026-11-13 14:30 UTC. "Tail" means every build-list row of priorities 1 to 3 that is not verified at the freeze time. "Version" means one tables directory with its own hash: `eic_v1`, then `eic_v1_1`, `eic_v1_2`, ... (written v1.1, v1.2 in prose). "Ladder version" and "last version" are defined in section B.

**PI ruling 38 (2026-11-13), applied.** In his words: "can you just freeze whatever we can get in the next 6h and then send out reliable watchdogs to wait for the remaining runs and as soon as they are finished you update the dataset with notes". Decision (s) is answered. This ruling replaces the sentence "after the tables pass they are frozen: no chunk edits a file in `$T1` again" by a version rule: no version is ever edited; an update is a new version beside the old one. It also replaces the stop rule of chunk D6f (all 45 validation tracks, at least 259 of 267 training tracks) for the freeze of `eic_v1`: the freeze takes what is verified at the freeze time.

**What was verified (2026-11-13), in code and on Nibi.**
- The known tail, read from `$B` on Nibi. Training tracks, DNase-seq: rows 132 (C18M02, H9), 216 (C45M02, testis), 232 (C20M02, heart left ventricle), 337 (C27M02, mesenchymal stem cell), 381 (C49M02, uterus), 385 (C35M02, omental fat pad), 387 (C33M02, occipital lobe), 389 (C19M02, HAP-1), 391 (C41M02, SK-MEL-5), 396 (C15M02, G401). Validation tracks, DNase-seq: rows 56 (C50M02, vagina; gets a 7-day run) and 57 (C34M02, OCI-LY7; running). Validation rows 46 (C25M21, H3K4me2) and 52 (C25M26, H3K9ac) are resubmitted now and may or may not verify by the freeze time. The count of missing training tracks is at least 10 if the whole DNase-seq tail is out, plus every non-tail row that is not verified by the freeze time (training rows 119, 120, 123, 124, 211 and 273 still ran at the review); it is not a bound.
- What the tail holds (ledger). DNase-seq has 34 training and 3 validation tracks (C03M02, C34M02, C50M02). C19M02 is the only non-control training track of HAP-1, a blind cell with 6 blind tracks (C19M16, 17, 18, 20, 22, 29); HAP-1 also has the training control C19CTL. The 10 cells hold 8 validation tracks (H9 2, mesenchymal stem cell 4, testis 1, heart left ventricle 1). Every tail experiment's `se` row (priority 3 rows 398, 400, 401, 402, 406, 408, 411, 413, 414, 417; priority 1 rows 100, 101) is COMPLETED.
- The scorable set (pinned interface D): an assay needs at least 3 validation tracks from 3 cells, each with halves. DNase-seq, H3K4me2 and H3K9ac have exactly 3 each. So on `eic_v1` DNase-seq is not imputation-scorable (1 of 3 validation tracks), and H3K4me2 or H3K9ac is not if row 46 or 52 is missing. The expected scorable set on `eic_v1` is 7 to 9 assays, against 10 on a complete dataset.
- Eval regions are a function of the genome mask and the seed only: `candidate_blocks` (`tools/onion_eval/evalset.py:217-236`) and `draw_regions` (`:279-285`), called at `:459` and `:467`; E7a passes no `--regions-bed`. Targets, inputs, pairs and `tables_sha256` come from the tables. So every version's eval set has the same regions.
- `ag2_fetch.py fetch` and `status` read only `region_bins` and `regions` of the `--evalset` file (`tools/onion_data/ag2_fetch.py:200-202`); both need `--outputs chip_histone,dnase,atac`.
- `build_tables.py build` keeps a row only for a verified measurement directory and lists every other expected token in `missing.tsv` with its reason (`:7-18`); its output check accepts a directory that holds only `withheld.tsv` (`check_out`, `:467-478`). With `--superset-of <older tables>` every line of the older `measurements.tsv` and `pairs.tsv` must reappear byte-identical, `withheld.tsv` must be identical and every covariate vocabulary must extend the older one with the ids kept; else nothing is written. `check_dataset.py --superset-of` checks the same (`:463`). This is the mechanism of the updates; no new code is needed. The code has no `--as-of` option: the as-of rule is the start time of the one build command (section B.1).
- The hash chain: `tables_sha256` covers `measurements.tsv, pairs.tsv, withheld.tsv, covariates.json, scorable.tsv, missing.tsv` (`tools/minicandi/dataset.py:175-176, 222-229`). `score.py` refuses an eval set whose hash differs from the tables on disk, and on validation a run whose checkpoint hash differs (`:57-60, 524-529`). `select_rule.py` requires one `evalset_sha256` and one `tables_sha256` over the arms it compares (`:25`); `arm_report.py` refuses a mix (`:226-236`). So the code already refuses a comparison across versions.
- Nibi allows one job of 7 days (`cpubase_bycore_b5`, `MaxTime=7-00:00:00`); the 3-day limit is band b4.

**A. What runs when.**

| work | when | why |
|---|---|---|
| ERg eval regions | now | regions read the genome mask and the seed only |
| AGxf AlphaGenome 2 fetch | now, after ERg | the fetch reads regions only |
| D6b collect | 2026-11-13 12:30 UTC, on the rows that are done then | `pending` and `verify` work per row |
| D6f freeze of `eic_v1` | the freeze time, 14:30 UTC | ruling 38 |
| E7a, E7b, QCa, RCa, BRa, L1a and the ladder | after D6f, as planned, on `eic_v1` | they need a version and its hash |
| OXa, then D6c batch 1 | after D6f | they only add rows; rows of tail experiments are left out |
| D6u update | per watchdog report | ruling 38 |
| interim tables `eic_v1_pre`, guard G6, probes E7p and L1p, smoke RCs, early collect D6p | deleted | the real freeze is 6 hours away; a code chunk with its review would not finish before it, and L1a keeps its own timing probe |

**B. The version design.**

**B.1 `eic_v1`, the freeze at the freeze time.** One `build_tables.py build` command, started at the freeze time, makes `$T1`. As-of rule: a measurement directory enters if it exists and verifies when the command reads it; `DATASET_EIC_V1.md` records the command's start and end time (UTC) and the `squeue` listing at the start. A row that finishes later is in `missing.tsv` and enters the next version, never `eic_v1`. `missing.tsv` lists every absent token with the builder's reason; `DATASET_EIC_V1.md` adds, per absent track, the role, assay, cell, job id, state and the cause (slow `bwa sampe`, Java heap, truncated source file, signal pass not run). A validation track whose signal products are absent is kept and listed with `products_missing` (the builder's rule); it has no signal-space score until an update. The ladder starts on `eic_v1` at once.

**B.2 Updates.** `eic_v1_<k>` = `build_tables.py build` with the same arguments and `--superset-of` the previous version, written to `$O/tables/eic_v1_<k>` (a sibling, so `genome_dir` resolves the same genome). The previous version is never touched. Each update has `CHANGES.md` (in the vault and copied beside the tables as a file that the hash does not cover): the tracks added, with role, assay, cell and experiment; the cause of each delay; the provenance notes of each added measurement (`provenance.json::notes`: Java heap setting, truncated source, repaired read names); the tracks still missing; the old and the new `tables_sha256`; the scorable set before and after. An update is built only when one of these holds, so that versions stay few (at most about 3): (i) a validation tail row has verified with its signal products; (ii) `$TD` exists; (iii) the foreman needs the ladder version at gate G2b and tail rows have verified since the last version.

**B.3 The eval set is versioned the same way.** `$O/eval/eic_v1_<k>/evalset.json`, built with the arguments of E7a step 1; its regions must equal `$RG`. Its baselines are scored only when the version becomes the ladder version or the last version.

**B.4 What uses which version.**
- Rule: every comparison under D13 is made within one dataset version and one eval-set version. An arm is never compared with an incumbent that was trained or scored on another version. The code enforces it by the hashes (lines above).
- Three policies were weighed for an update that arrives during the ladder. (a) Keep the whole ladder on `eic_v1`, and move only the final design onward to the last version: cheapest (about 170 CPU-h), but every rung is selected without DNase-seq validation targets and with at least 10 fewer DNase-seq training tracks. (b) Switch at every rung boundary and retrain the incumbents each time: correct, but the cross-rung comparison then needs every rung's selected arm retrained, at each switch. (c) One switch point: the recommended policy.
- Recommended, policy (c). Rung 1 (chunks L1a, L1b) runs on `eic_v1`. The **ladder version** is fixed at gate G2b: it is the newest version that exists when rungs 2 to 6 are submitted (the foreman builds an update first if tail rows have verified, rule B.2 (iii); he does not wait for a running row). Rungs 2 to 6, the cross-rung comparison, mix-and-match, the evolution step, stage 2, stage 3 and the resolution arms all use the ladder version and its eval set. If the ladder version is not `eic_v1`, rung 1's settlements stay valid (each was made within `eic_v1`), and rung 1's selected arm is retrained and scored on the ladder version (3 seeds) so that chunk LC compares six rungs within one version. The **last version** is the version built when `$TD` exists, or the newest version when chunk FDa starts, whichever comes first. From FDa on, everything uses the last version: FDa trains every arm of its comparison on it, the incumbent design included; FDb's done rule on validation, FEa, the superset `eic_v2` (built with `--superset-of` the last version), the single blind pass, the AlphaGenome 2 comparison (AGx pack, AGva to AGz) and both reports. Updates after FDa starts are built and noted, and no model uses them.
- Cost of policy (c). Eval set and baselines of the ladder version: 2 + 28 + the average table 50 = 80 CPU-h. Rung 1's selected arm on the ladder version: 3 x (1.04 + at most 5.3) = at most 19 GPU-slice-h. Eval set, baselines, average table and layers of the last version: 130 CPU-h (the cost of E7a). The incumbent design on the last version in FDa: 3 x (6.5 + 6.6) = 39 CPU-h. Total about 250 CPU-h and 19 GPU-slice-h, i.e. 0.9% of the 29,200 CPU-h of Amendment 2b. If the ladder version is `eic_v1` or equals the last version, the matching part is not spent.
- Metrics on a version that lacks tracks. A metric is reported on the tracks present. An assay under the scorable minimum on that version (DNase-seq on `eic_v1`) is in `non_scorable_assays.tsv`, outside the macro averages and the composite, and its row carries the flag `not scorable on <version>: n of 3 validation tracks`. The accessibility-only regime has no item for a target whose cell has no DNase-seq or ATAC-seq training track in that version; the table prints the count of such targets. Both are rerun on the update that adds the tracks, when that update is the ladder version or the last version.
- The add-on tables of the second experiments (`eic_v1x`) are an add-on of one version (`build_info.json::addon_of_tables_sha256`). Chunk OXx builds the add-on of the ladder version, and chunk OXy's rerun for the final design uses an add-on of the last version; the directory name carries the version (`eic_v1_<k>x`).
- Reports state, for every table and figure, the dataset version, the eval-set version and the tracks that version lacks.

**B.5 Watchdog and the foreman's steps (chunk D6u).** The cluster-side watchdog writes `$TS` (one line per tail row: row, experiment, track, role, job id, state, stage, elapsed, limit) and `$TD` when no tail row is queued or running. It never submits and never deletes. On each report the foreman runs chunk D6u: a failed tail row is resubmitted once, from `$KF4`, with `ONION_TIME=7-00:00:00` and, after a Java heap error or above 900 million reads, `ONION_MEM=192000M`; verified rows go into the next update under rule B.2. Stop rule: a tail row that fails its 7-day run stays missing for good, recorded with its reason in `CHANGES.md` and in `tail_closed.tsv`. No job chain, no non-standard `bwa sampe` setting and no third run is used. If row 56 or 57 ends so, DNase-seq stays non-scorable in the last version and is reported in `non_scorable_assays.tsv`.

**C. Chunks.** Now, in parallel: V6, ERg; then AGxf. At 12:30 UTC: D6b (addendum). At 14:30 UTC: D6f (addendum). After D6f: the chunks already planned, and OXa then D6c batch 1 (addenda). Per watchdog report: D6u. File sets are disjoint: no chunk of this amendment owns code.

Conditions: `$AGD/audit/fetch_conditions.tsv` (chunk AGa). Three calls first, then `status`, then invocations of at most 2,000 calls under `nohup`, one at a time, until `status` prints `missing: 0`; the stop rules of AGx hold (6 hours of waits; 21,504 calls). Then the peak files, from the login node in the kit's Python: `python /project/def-maxwl/mforooz/onion_kit/main/tools/onion_eval/ag2_peaks.py fetch --ledger /project/def-maxwl/mforooz/ONION/eic/accession_ledger.tsv --roles train,val --out /project/def-maxwl/mforooz/ONION/ag2/peaks`. No pack is written and no truth is read. `FETCH.md` as in AGx, plus `ag2_fetch.regions_sha256` of `$RG`.
- Addendum to the foreman's brief of chunk AGx: its steps (2), (3) and (5) become one `status` call on the eval set of the last version, which must print `missing: 0` (the cache is keyed by window and the regions are equal). Step (4), the pack, is unchanged and reads the last version (section B.4).

Compute: planned 9,216 API calls, about 34 minutes including quota waits at Max's measured rate; worst case 21,504. Storage: 2.6 GB cache, about 2 GB of peak files. No sbatch job.
Test: `hpc run nibi 'module load gcc arrow/25.0.0 python/3.11 && source /project/def-maxwl/mforooz/ONION/envs/ag2/bin/activate && python /project/def-maxwl/mforooz/onion_kit/main/tools/onion_data/ag2_fetch.py status --conditions /project/def-maxwl/mforooz/ONION/ag2/audit/fetch_conditions.tsv --evalset /project/def-maxwl/mforooz/ONION/eval/regions_v1/regions.json --outputs chip_histone,dnase,atac --cache /project/def-maxwl/mforooz/ONION/ag2/cache'` prints `missing: 0` on every line, and `hpc run nibi 'grep -rlF -f ~/.secrets/alphagenome_api_key /project/def-maxwl/mforooz/ONION/ag2 | wc -l'` prints 0.

Steps (1) to (6) run on the rows that are done at that time: `align_views.py verify --measurements $M` on every directory that exists; `build_list.py pending` per priority; `paths_exercised.md` on the rows that are done (it names a path that only a tail row can show).
