Totals of the remaining work, with the planning factor and a small trunk: data track 407 + 14,920 + 786 + 677 + 132 + 177 + 175 = 17,274 CPU-h; Track B on the cluster 80 + 140 = 220; ladder 303 + 494 + 487 + 700 + 1,943 + 547 + 355 = 4,829; mix and match 236; evolution 3,266; stage 2 to reports 1,400 + 41 + 39 + 1,857 + 5 = 3,342. Sum: 17,274 + 220 + 4,829 + 236 + 3,266 + 3,342 = about 29,200 CPU-h and 150 + 51 + 40 + 1 = 242 GPU-slice-h. With scoring at the measured pilot cost (about 0.8 CPU-h per run) in place of the planning factor, the sum is about 26,700 CPU-h and 90 GPU-slice-h. If the design has an axial trunk, add about 911 (mix and match) + 911 (confirmations) + 5,300 (stage 2) + 300 (final models) = about 7,400 CPU-h. If rung 5 moves to the GPU, LCa's second scorings rise from 32 to 95 GPU-h. The 147 ladder runs alone: training 375 + 347.7 + 522.3 + 982.8 + 369 = 2,596.8 CPU-h + 22.8 GPU-h.
Evolution budget (PI ruling 23, proposed here): at most 3,000 CPU-h of proxy attempts over all recipes and at most 48 h of wall per recipe; one attempt costs about 5 CPU-h on a small trunk (2 seeds x (20,000 windows = 1.6 CPU-h + about 0.8 CPU-h of scoring on 32 regions)) and about 24 CPU-h on an axial trunk; at most 6 candidates are confirmed at the full budget.
Storage added: `eic_v2` measurements about 1,134 x 105 MB = 120 GB; staged reads of priority 4 at most 1,800 GB at a time (5,329 GB in all, in three batches); DNA embeddings 63 GB; genome-wide predictions 3 models x (50 GB signal + 25 GB counts) = 225 GB; all on Nibi `/scratch`. About 400 run directories under 50 MB each = 20 GB and about 4,000 files on Nibi `/project` (442 GiB and 173K files were free on 2026-11-11).
Wall time on the critical path after `eic_v1` exists (expected 2026-11-13): E7a and E7b 1.5 days, rung 1 1 day, rungs 2 to 6 1.5 days, LCa and LC 1 day, mix and match 3 days, evolution 4 days, stage 2 2 days, final design 1 day, EIC model and blind pass 2 days, reports 1.5 days: about 18 to 19 days, so about 2026-12-02 if the Nibi queue holds. `eic_v2` (priority 4, three batches) is expected about 2026-11-20 and is needed only from gate G4.
What can start before `eic_v1` exists: the 18 chunks of wave B1 now; then B2 (RX, XE, A5x), B3 (S2K, A5c) and B4 (AS). D6b starts when the build's queue is empty. Everything else waits for the frozen tables.

**Risks added by amendments 2 and 2b, each with its cheap check.**
- The scoring cost on `eic_v1` is not measured (256 regions, halves and pairs), and the script's default time limit is 3 h. Check: `scoring_cost.tsv` in E7b and the first scoring job of L1a; `submit_arms.sh` pins the scoring `--time`.
- No CPU rung has been timed on Nibi. Check: the probe runs of L1a.
- Rungs are scored on different device types, and one comparison needs one device type. Check: the second scorings of LCa, LC and FDb.
- The read-derived QC columns may be empty in the build's rows. Check: `qc_coverage.tsv` in D6f; QCa and QCb fill them or report the D6 arm as infeasible with evidence.
- A stage-2 arm fails only on real data. Check: chunk AS runs every arm for 2,000 windows on `pilot_eic` before `eic_v1` exists.
- The frozen tables or the eval set change after runs exist, and `select_rule` then refuses every comparison. Check: `build_tables.py` refuses a non-empty output directory; side inputs (average table, QC table, DNA embeddings) live outside the tables directory.
- The pretrained DNA gain is leakage, and Flashzoi's training targets may include EIC blind experiments. Check: chunk FZ's overlap table (before any Flashzoi run); the leakage square in S2a and S2b; FDa keeps `dna_flashzoi` out of the EIC entry until the PI rules.
- The evolution search overfits the proxy (few regions, short training, 2 seeds). Check: `min_gain` = 2x the measured proxy noise; every preferred candidate is confirmed at the full budget with 3 seeds under D13; `proxy_against_full.tsv` reports how well the proxy predicted.
- An evolved module reaches data it must not read. Check: the proxy scorer's static checks (imports, config keys), `mode = val`, and the dataset's refusal of blind rows; the scorer and the dataset are outside the editable paths.
- A hybrid of the mix-and-match step cannot be built in one sitting. Check: MXp's module specification; MXh returns `blocked` for that combination and the others proceed.
- Testbed arm pairs share reads between halves. Check: `pairs between two testbed arms in pairs.tsv: 0` in `check_dataset.py`.
- The only copy of the measurements is on Nibi `/scratch`. Check: D6f and D6g write the nearline tar, or state the purge rule.
- A blind track is read twice, or by a run outside the freeze list, or a blind job crashes. Check: `ledger_check.txt` in BPb; the foreman-only repeat rule of BPa.
- The stage-2 cost depends on the design's trunk (1,400 to 6,700 CPU-h). Check: the foreman reads the figure at gate G3b before S2a submits.
- Priority-4 staging overfills `/scratch`. Check: three batches of at most 1,800 GB; `du` and `quota -s` before each batch.

## Decisions needed

- (a) Resolved by PI ruling 21. (b) Resolved by PI ruling 22. (c) Replaced by PI ruling 23 (mix and match, then evolution; chunks MXp to EVc). (d) Replaced by PI ruling 24 (the blind pass runs once also after a validation failure). (e) Confirmed by PI ruling 25.
- (f) May a design that contains the Flashzoi DNA source be the EIC-comparison entry? Flashzoi was trained on ENCODE tracks that may include EIC blind experiments. Recommended: no, if chunk FZ finds any EIC blind experiment among the Borzoi training targets; the Flashzoi arm then stays a reported ablation and the EIC entry uses the best design without it. Needed by chunk FDa; evidence in `results/<T:FZ>/FLASHZOI_LEAKAGE.md`.
- (o) to (r) (Amendment 5, AG2): resolved by PI rulings 34 to 37 and by the PI's reading of the Terms of Service (2026-11-13).
- (s) (Amendment 6, the freeze of `eic_v1`): resolved by PI ruling 38 (freeze at 2026-11-13 14:30 UTC with what is verified; watched tail; versioned updates with notes).
- (t), (u), (v) (Amendment 7, regions): resolved by PI rulings 39 (no protocol change now; one exclusion arm, the gap reported), 40 (leak-free strata only; no cCRE or ChromHMM strata now) and 41 (512 evaluation regions on evidence; 1,024 needs the PI).
